step 1: build (default) -j 1
  run 0 2 w0 out/m0/f0.txt indent(2)
  run 2 4 w0 out/m0/f1.txt indent(2)
  run 4 6 w0 out/m0/f2.txt upper()
  run 6 8 w0 out/m0/bundle.txt count_lines()
  run 8 9 w0 out/m0/wrapped.txt wrap("<m0|", "|>\n")
  run 9 13 w0 out/m1/f0.txt sort_lines()
  run 13 15 w0 out/m1/f1.txt lower()
  run 15 19 w0 out/m1/f2.txt sort_lines()
  run 19 23 w0 out/m1/bundle.txt concat()
  run 23 27 w0 out/m1/wrapped.txt wrap("<m1|", "|>\n")
  run 27 29 w0 out/m1/doc.txt preprocess()
  run 29 31 w0 out/m2/f0.txt upper()
  run 31 33 w0 out/m2/f1.txt upper()
  run 33 34 w0 out/m2/f2.txt wrap("[", "]\n")
  run 34 37 w0 out/m2/bundle.txt hash()
  run 37 37 w0 out/m2/wrapped.txt wrap("<m2|", "|>\n")
  run 37 40 w0 out/all.txt count_lines()
  ok ran=17 hit=0 time=40
